    "id": "İn iyəsi adalah roh penjaga gua dalam kepercayaan tradisional masyarakat Turkik dan Altay.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kosmologi masyarakat Turkik dan Altay, İn iyəsi dipandang sebagai roh penunggu gua tempat setiap rongga bumi dinaungi oleh entitas tersendiri.",
      "en": "In Turkic and Altay folk cosmology, İn iyəsi represents the protective spirit of caves where each cavern has its own guardian.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "in-iy-si-c01",
        "in-iy-si-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Gua dipercaya sebagai lorong sakral penghubung antardunia dan alam rahasia, sehingga keberadaan penjaga gua dihormati oleh peziarah dan pengelana.",
      "en": "Caves serve as sacred threshold passages linking mortal lands with hidden realms, earning these cavern guardians deep reverence.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "in-iy-si-c01",
        "in-iy-si-c05"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "İsa-Musa quşu adalah sepasang burung mitos jelmaan dua bersaudara yang mencari lembu gembalaan mereka dalam cerita rakyat Azerbaijan.",
    "en": "İsa-Musa quşu are mythical birds originating from two transformed brothers searching for lost cattle in Azerbaijani folklore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "isa-musa-qusu-c01",
      "isa-musa-qusu-c02",
      "isa-musa-qusu-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Menurut legenda Azerbaijan, Isa dan Musa adalah dua saudara pengembala yang tertidur di padang rumput hingga kawanan ternak mereka hilang tanpa jejak.",
      "en": "According to Azerbaijani lore, Isa and Musa were two herder brothers who fell asleep on the pasture and lost their cattle.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "isa-musa-qusu-c01",
        "isa-musa-qusu-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Karena takut akan amukan majikan atau ibu tiri, mereka memohon agar diubah menjadi burung lalu terbang mencari lembu mereka sepanjang malam.",
      "en": "Fearing their harsh master or stepmother, they prayed for transformation into birds to search tirelessly across the wilderness.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "isa-musa-qusu-c03",
        "isa-musa-qusu-c04",
        "isa-musa-qusu-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Kisah ini melambangkan duka persaudaraan dan penyesalan, dikaitkan dengan suara sahut-sahutan burung hantu malam di desa-desa Azerbaijan.",
    "en": "The legend personifies brotherhood and sorrow, explaining the mournful nocturnal cries of small owls in Azerbaijani villages.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "isa-musa-qusu-c01",
      "isa-musa-qusu-c05"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "İsa-Musa quşu",

    "literal_meaning": {
      "id": "burung Isa dan Musa",
      "en": "bird of Isa and Musa"
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "isa-musa-qusu-c01"
    ]
  },

      "id": "Dua pemuda gembala yang bertransformasi menjadi burung nokturnal.",
      "en": "Two pastoral brothers transformed into nocturnal birds.",

        "isa-musa-qusu-c04"
      ]

      ]

    "id": "Legenda ini sering dikaitkan oleh masyarakat Azerbaijan dengan suara khas burung hantu celepuk hutan.",
    "en": "Azerbaijani folklore associates the mournful calls of scops owls with the calls between Isa and Musa.",

      "isa-musa-qusu-c01"
    ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c01",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "İsa-Musa quşu və ya İsaq-Musaq quşu— meşə bayquşcuğu ilə bağlı azərbaycanlılarda yayılmış mif.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "İsa-Musa quşu atau İsaq-Musaq quşu adalah mitos masyarakat Azerbaijan seputar burung hantu hutan kecil.",
        "en": "İsa-Musa quşu or İsaq-Musaq quşu is an Azerbaijani myth associated with the scops owl."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c02",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "İsa və Musa adında iki qardaş çöldə öküzlərini (və ya inəklərini) otararkən yuxuya gedirlər və oyananda heyvanlarını tapa bilmirlər.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dua saudara bernama Isa dan Musa tertidur saat menggembala di padang terbuka dan mendapati hewan mereka hilang saat bangun.",
        "en": "Two brothers named Isa and Musa fell asleep while herding oxen and could not find their animals upon waking."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c03",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "Əzazil ağalarının (və ya ögey analarının) qorxusundan Allaha yalvarıb quşa çevrilmələrini istəyirlər.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena takut pada majikan atau ibu tiri mereka, kedua bersaudara memohon agar diubah menjadi burung.",
        "en": "Out of fear of their harsh master or stepmother, the brothers prayed to be transformed into birds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c04",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "Arzuları yerinə yetir və quş şəklinə keçərək öküzləri axtarmağa başlayırlar.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Harapan mereka terkabul dan mereka berubah menjadi burung untuk mencari ternak yang hilang.",
        "en": "Their wish was granted, transforming into birds to search for the missing cattle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c05",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "”Həmin vaxtdan Azərbaycan kəndlərinin hər yerində İsa-Musa quşları peyda oldu.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak saat itu burung İsa-Musa terdengar bermunculan di seluruh desa-desa Azerbaijan.",
        "en": "Since that time, İsa-Musa birds appeared throughout villages across Azerbaijan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isa-musa-qusu-c06",
      "source_id": "isa-musa-qusu-s1",
      "quote": "Mifin anadil quşları və Yapalaq quşu üçün versiyaları da vardır.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitos ini juga memiliki versi yang dikaitkan dengan burung anadil dan burung yapalaq.",
        "en": "The myth also has versions associated with anadil birds and yapalaq owls."
      }
    }
  ],

        "id": "Kepercayaan ini terbatas pada daerah tertentu dan tidak tersebar ke seluruh masyarakat Turkmen.",
        "en": "However, this belief is restricted to certain localities and not widespread among all Turkmen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "karak-rnak-c06",
      "source_id": "karak-rnak-s1",
      "quote": "Onun için en eski zamanlarda çocuklarının suda oynamasından ve boğulacaklarından korkan anneler, onları \"Karakırnak\" ile korkuturlardı.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak zaman dahulu, para ibu menakut-nakuti anak-anak dengan Karakırnak agar mereka tidak bermain di air dan tenggelam.",
        "en": "Mothers frightened children with Karakırnak so that they would not play in water and drown."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kayış atau Kayış Baldır adalah gulyabani berkaki tali kulit yang mengelabui pengelana untuk menggendongnya.",
    "en": "Kayış or Kayış Baldır is a strap-legged ghoul that tricks travelers into carrying it on their shoulders.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kay-s-c01",
      "kay-s-c02",
      "kay-s-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat, Kayış digambarkan berwujud kakek tak berdaya yang duduk di tepi sungai meminta tolong diseberangkan.",
      "en": "In folk traditions, Kayış manifests as a seemingly helpless old man by riverbanks asking to be carried across.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kay-s-c01",
        "kay-s-c03",
        "kay-s-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ketika dinaikkan ke pundak, sepasang kaki panjang lentur seperti tali kulit keluar melilit pinggang pengelana dan memperbudak korbannya.",
      "en": "Once hoisted onto a traveler's shoulders, long flexible legs resembling leather straps bind the victim's waist tightly.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kay-s-c02",
        "kay-s-c04",
        "kay-s-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Cerita ini dituturkan kepada anak-anak untuk memperingatkan bahaya keluar rumah saat gelap malam setelah waktu azan magrib.",
    "en": "Told as a pedagogical warning tale, this legend discouraged children from wandering outside in the dark after evening prayer.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kay-s-c01",
      "kay-s-c06"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Kayış",
    "language": "tr",
    "literal_meaning": {
      "id": "tali kulit atau sabuk lentur",
      "en": "leather strap or belt"
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "kay-s-c02"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {

      "id": "Ghoul penipu dengan kaki lentur menyerupai tali kulit.",
      "en": "Deceptive ghoul with legs like flexible leather straps.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kay-s-c02"
      ]
    },
    "origin": {

      ]

      "en": "Ensnaring travelers and wrapping its legs about their bodies.",

      "en": "Its strap-like appendages that tightly bind victims.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kay-s-c02",
        "kay-s-c05"
      ]
    }
  },

    "en": "According to folk tales, Kayış emerges only after the evening call to prayer has sounded.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kay-s-c06"

  },
  "abilities": [],
  "weaknesses": [],
  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "kay-s-s1",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c01",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "Kayış, Çocukları korkutmak için uydurulmuş kötücül varlıktır.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kayış adalah makhluk jahat yang diceritakan untuk menakut-nakuti anak-anak.",
        "en": "Kayış is a malevolent creature invented to scare children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c02",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "Kayış Baldır veya Kayış Bacak da denilen, ayakları kayıştan gulyabanidir.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dikenal pula sebagai Kayış Baldır atau Kayış Bacak, sesosok gulyabani berkaki tali kulit.",
        "en": "Also called Kayış Baldır or Kayış Bacak, it is a ghoul whose legs are made of leather straps."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c03",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "O, kendi ayakları üzerinde durup yürüyemez.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tidak dapat berdiri atau berjalan di atas kakinya sendiri.",
        "en": "It cannot stand or walk on its own legs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c04",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "Bunun için de yoldan giden insanları aldatıp, onun boynuna biner.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena itu ia memperdaya orang yang lewat di jalanan untuk menaikkannya ke leher mereka.",
        "en": "Because of this, it deceives passersby into letting it ride upon their necks."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c05",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "Sonra kayış ayaklarını onun beline dolayıp, yolu bitinceye kadar onu bırakmaz.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kemudian ia melilitkan kaki tali kulitnya ke pinggang korban dan tidak melepaskannya hingga perjalanan selesai.",
        "en": "Then it wraps its strap legs around their waist and does not release them until the journey ends."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kay-s-c06",
      "source_id": "kay-s-s1",
      "quote": "O, akşam ezanından sonra ortaya çıkar.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini muncul berkeliaran setelah azan magrib.",
        "en": "It emerges after the evening call to prayer."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kayberen adalah roh penolong penguasa pegunungan dalam kepercayaan Kirgiz yang melindungi satwa pemamah biak.",
    "en": "Kayberen are benevolent mountain guardian spirits in Kyrgyz folklore protecting ruminant wild animals.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kayberen-c01",
      "kayberen-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Menurut kepercayaan rakyat Kirgiz, Kayberen mendiami puncak-puncak gunung berbatu dan memelihara kelestarian hewan liar pemamah biak.",
      "en": "In Kyrgyz tradition, Kayberen inhabit rocky alpine peaks and oversee the wellbeing of wild grazing animals.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kayberen-c01",
        "kayberen-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Para pemburu selalu memohon doa restu kepada roh ini sebelum berburu agar terhindar dari marabahaya di tebing jurang gunung.",
      "en": "Hunters invoked their favor before mountain expeditions to ensure safety on hazardous crags and successful sustenance.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kayberen-c03",
        "kayberen-c04",
        "kayberen-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Konsep Kayberen mengatur etika berburu tradisional Kirgiz agar manusia tidak membantai hewan secara berlebihan dan menghormati alam.",
    "en": "Kayberen ethics regulated nomadic hunting practices, preventing over-hunting and instilling respect for highland wildlife.",

      "id": "kayberen-c01",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Kayberen, Kırgızlar'ın iyiliksever ruhlar arasına dahil ettikleri ve \"kayıp eren\" adıyla andıkları ruhlardır, dağlarda yaşarlar ve geviş getiren hayvanları korurlar.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kayberen adalah roh penolong dalam kepercayaan bangsa Kirgiz yang tinggal di pegunungan dan melindungi hewan pemamah biak.",
        "en": "Kayberen are benevolent spirits in Kyrgyz belief residing in mountains to protect ruminant animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kayberen-c02",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Kırgızlar'ın inancına göre bu ruhlar, hayvanların artıp çoğalmasını sağlar.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan Kirgiz, roh-roh ini menjaga kelimpahan dan perkembangbiakan hewan.",
        "en": "According to Kyrgyz belief, these spirits enable animals to multiply and thrive."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kayberen-c03",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Ancak kızdıkları zaman da hayvanları telef edebilir.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun apabila mereka marah, roh ini juga dapat membinasakan kawanan hewan.",
        "en": "However, when angered, they can perish the animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kayberen-c04",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Bunun için de ava çıkmadan önce, uğurlu geçmesi için \"kayberen\"den yardım istenir.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Oleh sebab itu, sebelum pergi berburu dimohonkan bantuan kepada Kayberen demi keselamatan dan keberhasilan.",
        "en": "For this reason, before hunting, assistance is requested from Kayberen for good fortune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kayberen-c05",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Dağlarda, taşlarda yaşayan ve hayvanları koruyan bu ruhlar aynı zamanda yaşadıkları yerin iyesidirler.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh yang tinggal di gunung dan bebatuan ini sekaligus berperan sebagai roh penunggu atau penguasa tempat tersebut.",
        "en": "Living in mountains and rocks, these protective spirits are also the masters of their domain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kayberen-c06",
      "source_id": "kayberen-s1",
      "quote": "Çiltanlar (Kırklar)da Kayberenler gibi dağda yaşayıp hayvanları korurlardı.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para Çiltan juga hidup di gunung dan menjaga hewan seperti halnya Kayberen.",

      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kuu adalah angsa sakral dan burung totemik dalam kepercayaan kuno masyarakat Kirgiz dan suku-suku Turkik.",
    "en": "Kuu is a sacred swan and ancestral totem bird in the traditional beliefs of the Kyrgyz and Turkic peoples.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kuu-c01",
      "kuu-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kosmologi tradisional Kirgiz, Kuu atau angsa putih dihormati sebagai burung totem suci (ongon) yang telah dipuja sejak era kuno suku Xiongnu.",
      "en": "In Kyrgyz folk cosmology, Kuu or the white swan is revered as a sacred totemic bird (ongon) venerated since ancient Xiongnu times.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kuu-c01",
        "kuu-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Unggas suci ini dipandang sebagai pelindung garis keturunan ibu, sehingga terdapat tabu ketat yang melarang membunuh atau memakan dagingnya.",
      "en": "Regarded as a guardian of maternal kin, hunting or consuming the sacred swan was strictly forbidden under ancestral taboos.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "kuu-c04",
        "kuu-c05",
        "kuu-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Tradisi memuliakan Kuu berkaitan erat dengan mitos asal-usul nenek moyang dan penghormatan terhadap garis kekerabatan pihak ibu.",
    "en": "Reverence for Kuu is linked to ancestral ethnogenetic myths and deep veneration for maternal lineage in nomadic Siberian-Turkic lore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kuu-c01",
      "kuu-c02",
      "kuu-c06"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {

      "id": "Menjaga kesejahteraan keluarga dan mendampingi garis keturunan ibu.",

      "en": "Strict sacred taboos against hunting or harming the bird.",

        "kuu-c04",

      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Di kalangan suku Yakut, memakan daging burung Kuu merupakan pantangan adat yang sangat dilarang keras.",
    "en": "Among the Yakut people, eating the meat of the Kuu was strictly forbidden under tribal custom.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "abilities": [],
  "weaknesses": [],
  "sources": [

      "id": "kuu-s1",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kuu-c01",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Куу же ак куу (кара куу) - кыргыздардын түшүнүктөрүндөгү ыйык, тотемдик куш.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuu atau angsa putih merupakan burung suci dan burung totem dalam kepercayaan bangsa Kirgiz.",
        "en": "Kuu or the swan is a sacred totemic bird in Kyrgyz beliefs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuu-c02",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Куу - эзелки хуннуларда, түрк тилдүү урууларда сыйынуучу канаттуу-онгон болгондугун аныктаган фактылар бар.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuu merupakan burung pemujaan dan ongon di kalangan suku-suku Xiongnu dan Turkik kuno.",
        "en": "Kuu was an ongon bird of veneration among ancient Xiongnu and Turkic tribes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuu-c03",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Ортончу уулу ак кууга айланып учуп кеткен имиш.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda, putra tengah dikisahkan berubah menjadi angsa putih lalu terbang menjauh.",
        "en": "In legend, the middle son transformed into a white swan and flew away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuu-c04",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Уруктун өкүлдөрүнө ак кууну өлтүрүүгө, ага зыян келтирүүгө табу салынган.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Terdapat pantangan adat bagi anggota marga untuk membunuh atau mencelakai burung angsa putih.",
        "en": "A taboo prohibited clan members from killing or harming the white swan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuu-c05",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Якуттарда куунун этин жегенге тыюу салынган.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di kalangan orang Yakut, memakan daging angsa dilarang keras oleh adat.",
        "en": "Among the Yakuts, eating the flesh of the swan was forbidden."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuu-c06",
      "source_id": "kuu-s1",
      "quote": "Куу - эне тараптагы туугандардын сактоочусу милдетин аткарган.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuu menjalankan fungsi sebagai penjaga bagi kerabat dari pihak garis keturunan ibu.",
        "en": "Kuu performed the role of protector of maternal kin."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

      "m-gen-c05"
    ]

  "long_description": [

      "id": "Dalam kisah epos Nart, Əməgen digambarkan sebagai raksasa bertubuh buruk rupa yang terkadang memiliki banyak kepala dan berkembang biak dengan sangat cepat.",
      "en": "In the Nart sagas, Əməgen are depicted as grotesque giants who occasionally possess multiple heads and multiply rapidly.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "m-gen-c01",
        "m-gen-c02",
        "m-gen-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Para ksatria Nart terus-menerus bertempur melawan keganasan mereka, sebab raksasa ini memangsa manusia dan dipandang sebagai sumber segala malapetaka di dunia.",
      "en": "Nart heroes fought continually against their brutality, as these giants devoured captives and were regarded as the source of worldly evils.",

        "m-gen-c05",
        "m-gen-c06"

    "en": "The Əməgen personify the untamed savagery of the high Caucasus mountains and stand as the perennial foes of Nart champions.",

      "source_id": "m-gen-s1",
      "quote": "Nart qəhrəmanları davamlı emegenlerle vuruşub və gücləriylə, zəkalarıyla onları hər vaxt yenmeyi bacararlar.",

      "en": "In Altay shamanism and Yörük nomadic traditions, Markut is envisioned as a celestial eagle with copper claws accompanying the sky god Ülgen.",

    {
      "id": "markut-c01",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Markut, Merküt veya Bürküt, Yörükler arasında yaramazlık yapan çocukları korkutmak için uydurulan efsanevi kuş.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Markut, Merküt, atau Bürküt adalah burung legendaris di kalangan masyarakat Yörük.",
        "en": "Markut, Merküt, or Bürküt is a legendary bird known among the Yörüks."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "markut-c02",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Eski inanışa göre, Markut sadece adı anılanları korkutan bir kuştur.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan lama, Markut adalah burung yang menakuti siapa saja yang namanya disebut.",
        "en": "According to ancient belief, Markut is a bird that frightens those whose names are invoked."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "markut-c03",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Kötü ruhları korkutmak amacıyla çağırılan Markut adındaki bu kuş ile Türkmenlerdeki Bürküt Ata ve Yakutların, şamanların Bürküt soyundan gelme inançları arasında mitolojik anlamsallık açısından yakınlık bulunmaktadır.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Burung Markut dipanggil untuk menakuti roh-roh jahat dan berkaitan erat dengan figur Bürküt Ata.",
        "en": "The bird Markut is invoked to frighten evil spirits and is closely connected to Bürküt Ata."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "markut-c04",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Radlov'un sözünü ettiği Markut, göze görünmez ve kötü ruhları korkutmak için çağırılır.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Markut tak kasat mata dan dipanggil dalam doa saman untuk mengusir roh-roh jahat.",
        "en": "Markut is invisible and summoned to frighten evil spirits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "markut-c05",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Bu kuşu ancak belli insanlar tarafından görüşebilir.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Burung sakral ini hanya dapat disaksikan oleh orang-orang tertentu yang memiliki kemampuan gaib.",
        "en": "This bird can only be seen by specific people endowed with special vision."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "markut-c06",
      "source_id": "markut-s1",
      "quote": "Teleüt şamanlarının davullarında yer alan resimlerden biri de \"Ülgen kuşu Bay Mörküt\" adında büyük kartal görünüşlü bir kuştur.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada tambur saman Teleüt, burung ini dilukiskan berwujud elang besar pendamping dewa Ülgen bernama Bay Mörküt.",
        "en": "On the drums of Teleut shamans, one depiction is a large eagle-like bird called Bay Mörküt, companion of Ülgen."
      }
    }

        "id": "Nihang yang paling terkenal hidup di sebuah danau di wilayah Mastuj dan menakuti penduduk setempat.",
        "en": "The most famous one lived in a lake in the Mastuj area of Chitral and frightened local people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nihang-mythology-c06",
      "source_id": "nihang-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "However, its reign of terror ended when an ancient warrior fought against it using a double-hilted sword and killed it.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Masa terornya berakhir saat seorang ksatria kuno bertarung menggunakan pedang berhulu ganda dan membunuhnya.",
        "en": "Its terror ended when an ancient warrior fought it using a double-hilted sword and killed it."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

      "quote": "Пир (иранча эски, кары, билерман кары) — түрк тилдүү элдердин мифологиясында ар кандай иштин колдоочусу.",

      "id": "pir-c04",
      "source_id": "pir-s1",
      "quote": "Элдер ошол П-лердин атына сыйынган, алар колдойт деп ишенишкен.",

      "en": "In Anatolian folklore and Turkic belief, Ərbükə are intelligent serpent hybrids having human upper bodies and snake tails below the waist.",

      "en": "Regarded as the daughter of the Sea King, their appearance is traditionally interpreted as an unlucky omen foretelling aquatic disaster.",

      "source_id": "susulu-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Susulus appear in Turkic folklore as unlucky omens, both foretelling disaster and provoking it like mermaids.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Susulu muncul dalam cerita rakyat Turkik sebagai pertanda buruk pembawa bencana.",
        "en": "Susulus appear in Turkic folklore as unlucky omens, foretelling and provoking disaster."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "susulu-mythology-c04",
      "source_id": "susulu-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "They are conventionally depicted as beautiful creatures with long flowing hair.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka biasanya digambarkan sebagai makhluk berparas jelita dengan rambut panjang terurai.",
        "en": "They are conventionally depicted as beautiful creatures with long flowing hair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "susulu-mythology-c05",
      "source_id": "susulu-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "They are said to inhabit lakes and rivers.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka dikatakan mendiami danau dan sungai.",
        "en": "They are said to inhabit lakes and rivers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "susulu-mythology-c06",
      "source_id": "susulu-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Susulus have also been described as able to swim up rivers to freshwater lakes.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Susulu juga digambarkan mampu berenang menyusuri sungai menuju danau air tawar.",
        "en": "Susulus have also been described as able to swim up rivers to freshwater lakes."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Unuqay adalah monster pengubah wujud kambing dalam cerita rakyat Azerbaijan yang memperdaya pengelana malam.",
    "en": "Unuqay is a shapeshifting goat monster in Azerbaijani folklore that tricks nighttime travelers on isolated roads.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "unuqay-c01",
      "unuqay-c02",
      "unuqay-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Azerbaijan, Unuqay dipandang sebagai manifestasi pengubah wujud (keçi dönərgəsi) yang berhubungan erat dengan sosok Hal anası, menampakkan diri berwujud kambing liar tanpa pemilik.",
      "en": "In Azerbaijani lore, Unuqay is considered a shapeshifter manifestation (keçi donərgəsi) tied to Hal anasi, presenting itself as an unowned stray goat.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "unuqay-c01",
        "unuqay-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Entitas ini kerap berkeliaran di dekat kuburan saat hari mulai gelap dan memperdaya penunggang kuda sehingga korbannya menderita gagap atau kelumpuhan, tetapi ia takut pada jarum dan peniti.",
      "en": "Lurking near graveyards at nightfall, it deceives riders leading to long-lasting afflictions such as stutters, yet fears needles and pins.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "unuqay-c02",
        "unuqay-c03",
        "unuqay-c04",
        "unuqay-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Sosok Unuqay mencerminkan peringatan folklor pedesaan agar tidak sembarangan mengambil atau memungut hewan liar asing yang ditemukan di jalan pada malam hari.",
    "en": "Unuqay embodies traditional pastoral cautions against taking unknown wandering livestock encountered after dusk.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "unuqay-c01",
      "unuqay-c02",
      "unuqay-c06"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Unuqay",

    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {

      "en": "Deceptive goat monster misleading nighttime road travelers.",

    "id": "Meskipun memiliki kekuatan jahat yang melumpuhkan manusia, Unuqay dapat dihalau hanya dengan menusukkan jarum atau peniti pakaian.",
    "en": "Despite its debilitating malevolence, Unuqay can be repelled by simply brandishing ordinary sewing needles.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c01",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "Unuqay və ya Hal anasının keçi dönərgəsi — Azərbaycan mifologiyasında bədheybət.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Unuqay, atau perwujudan kambing dari Hal anası, adalah monster dalam mitologi Azerbaijan.",
        "en": "Unuqay, or the goat manifestation of Hal anasi, is a monster in Azerbaijani mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c02",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "Atlıların və arabaçıların qarşısına keçi formasında çıxaraq onları aldadır və uzun müddət davam edəcək tibbi qüsurlara (kəkələmə, axsama) səbəb olur.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menipu penunggang kuda dan pengemudi kereta dengan menampakkan diri sebagai kambing serta menyebabkan cacat fisik jangka panjang.",
        "en": "It deceives riders and cart drivers by appearing as a goat, causing long-lasting afflictions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c03",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "Hal anası kimi iynə-sancaqdan qorxur.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti halnya Hal anası, makhluk ini takut pada jarum dan peniti.",
        "en": "Like Hal anasi, it is afraid of needles and pins."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c04",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "Unuqay qəbiristanlıq ətrafı ərazilərdə görülür.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Unuqay sering terlihat berkeliaran di kawasan sekitar pemakaman.",
        "en": "Unuqay is often sighted around cemetery perimeters."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c05",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "O, əsasən axşamüstü, qaranlıq çökərkən insanların rastına çıxır.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini terutama menampakkan diri kepada manusia pada petang hari saat kegelapan turun.",
        "en": "It primarily encounters humans in the evening as darkness falls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unuqay-c06",
      "source_id": "unuqay-s1",
      "quote": "Unuqay çox zaman yoldan keçən atlıya və ya arabalı yolçuya sahibi olmayan keçi kimi görünür.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Unuqay kerap terlihat seperti kambing tak bertuan bagi penunggang kuda atau pelancong berkereta.",
        "en": "Unuqay often appears as an ownerless goat to passing horsemen or travelers in carts."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Achbuudan adalah kuda perang tulpar sakti milik pahlawan Joloy dalam tradisi epos Kirgiz.",
    "en": "Achbuudan is the legendary battle tulpar of epic hero Joloy celebrated for its loyalty and speed.",
    "claim_ids": [

      "wd-q13166364-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam epos Kirgiz, Achbuudan digambarkan sebagai kuda tempur sekaligus tulpar pacuan andalan pahlawan Joloy yang menempati peringkat kedua tepat di belakang Akkula dalam lomba pacuan akbar Kokotoy.",
      "en": "In Kyrgyz epic lore, Achbuudan served as hero Joloy's battle mount and racing tulpar, finishing right behind Akkula in Kokotoy's great feast race.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q13166364-c01",
        "wd-q13166364-c02",
        "wd-q13166364-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kuda perkasa ini berulang kali menyelamatkan Joloy dari maut, dan dalam salah satu varian epos ia bahkan dianugerahi kemampuan berbicara memohonkan ampun bagi tuannya di hadapan Manas.",
      "en": "This mighty mount continually rescued Joloy from peril, and in one variant gained the faculty of speech to plead for Joloy's life before Manas.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q13166364-c03",
        "wd-q13166364-c04",
        "wd-q13166364-c05"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bookholdoi adalah roh orang yang telah meninggal dunia dalam kepercayaan tradisional masyarakat Buryat.",
    "en": "Bookholdoi is a ghost or spirit of the deceased in traditional Buryat folk beliefs.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q19714498-c01",
      "wd-q19714498-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kosmologi shamanisme Buryat, khususnya di kalangan orang Buryat Irkutsk, jiwa seseorang yang wafat dipercaya bertransformasi menjadi roh gentayangan yang disebut bookholdoi.",
      "en": "In Buryat shamanic lore, particularly among the Irkutsk Buryats, the soul of a deceased person becomes a lingering spirit known as bookholdoi.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c01",
        "wd-q19714498-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Entitas tak kasatmata ini berdiam di antara alam kubur dan pemukiman manusia serta menjadi bagian penting dalam tradisi pemujaan arwah leluhur.",
      "en": "These invisible entities dwell near burial grounds and dwellings, remaining central to ancestor veneration and death rituals.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c02",
        "wd-q19714498-c05",
        "wd-q19714498-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Konsep bookholdoi mencerminkan pandangan animisme Siberia mengenai perjalanan jiwa manusia pascakematian.",
    "en": "The bookholdoi concept reflects Siberian animist understandings of the human soul's afterlife journey.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q19714498-c01",
      "wd-q19714498-c02"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Боохолдой",

      "en": "ghost or spirit of the deceased"
    },
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {
    "who": {
      "id": "Arwah orang mati yang gentayangan setelah akhir hayat.",
      "en": "Lingering soul of a deceased person following passing.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c01",
        "wd-q19714498-c03"
      ]
    },
    "origin": {

      "en": "Animist and shamanic lore of Siberian Buryats.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c01",
        "wd-q19714498-c04"
      ]
    },
    "role": {
      "id": "Menghubungkan orang yang masih hidup dengan arwah mereka yang telah tiada.",
      "en": "Bridging living descendants with departed ancestral spirits.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c02",
        "wd-q19714498-c05"
      ]
    },
    "famous_for": {
      "id": "Kelahirannya yang terjadi seketika saat jiwa manusia meninggalkan raga.",
      "en": "Arising immediately when a human soul departs the mortal body.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q19714498-c01",
        "wd-q19714498-c06"
      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Bagi masyarakat Buryat di wilayah Irkutsk, penyebutan bookholdoi secara khusus merujuk pada jiwa orang yang telah berpulang.",
    "en": "Among Irkutsk Buryats, the term bookholdoi specifically designates the soul of one who has passed away.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q19714498-c04"
    ]
  },

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Aruaq adalah roh leluhur pelindung dalam tradisi spiritual dan kepercayaan bangsa Kazakh.",
    "en": "Aruaq represents the revered ancestral protective spirits in Kazakh folk tradition and spirituality.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25506634-c01",
      "wd-q25506634-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Berdasarkan kepercayaan kuno bahwa roh hidup terpisah dari raga, bangsa Kazakh meyakini bahwa aruaq senantiasa menjaga dan membimbing keturunannya yang masih hidup di dunia.",
      "en": "Originating from the ancient concept that the soul exists separately from the body, Kazakhs believe the aruaq continually protects and guides the living.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25506634-c01",
        "wd-q25506634-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Masyarakat mendoakan arwah leluhur di dekat pekuburan, membawa tanah makam saat merantau, dan memohon kesembuhan serta keturunan melalui berkah aruaq.",
      "en": "People pray near cemeteries, carry grave soil when departing homeland, and seek healing and progeny through ancestral blessings.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25506634-c04",
        "wd-q25506634-c05",
        "wd-q25506634-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Pemujaan aruaq merupakan tiang utama identitas kekerabatan dan moralitas bangsa Kazakh lintas generasi.",
    "en": "Veneration of aruaq is a cornerstone of Kazakh lineage identity and moral solidarity across generations.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25506634-c01",
      "wd-q25506634-c02"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Аруақ",
    "language": "kk",

      "en": "spirit or ancestral soul"

    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25506634-c01"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {
    "who": {
      "id": "Roh leluhur suci pembimbing dan penjaga keturunan.",
      "en": "Sacred ancestral spirit guiding and protecting descendants.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25506634-c01",
        "wd-q25506634-c02"
      ]
    },
    "origin": {

      "en": "Animist cosmology and ancestor veneration of the Kazakhs.",

        "wd-q25506634-c01",
        "wd-q25506634-c03"
      ]

      "id": "Tradisi membawa segenggam tanah pemakaman leluhur sebagai pelindung saat merantau.",
      "en": "The sacred custom of carrying a handful of ancestral soil when leaving home.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25506634-c05"
      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Para ksatria Kazakh masa lampau berseru memanggil nama aruaq leluhur sebagai seruan perang penyemangat di garis depan pertempuran.",
    "en": "Ancient Kazakh warriors invoked the names of their ancestral aruaq as rallying war cries on the battlefield.",

    ]

  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-s1",

      "published": null,
      "language": "kk",
      "type": "wikipedia",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c01",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Аруақ  — Жан денеден бөлек өмір сүреді деген көне ұғымнан туған діни наным бойынша өлген кісінің тіршіліктегі адамдарды желеп-жебеп жүретін жаны (аруағы).",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aruaq adalah roh orang yang telah meninggal yang dipercaya senantiasa melindungi dan membimbing orang hidup.",
        "en": "Aruaq is the soul of a deceased person believed to protect and guide the living."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Халықта аруаққа, әсіресе тірісінде ел ағасы, ру басы, би, батыр болған қадірлі адамдарды қасиет тұтып, киелі санап, соның аруағына сыйыну салты кең тараған.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Masyarakat menghormati leluhur terkemuka seperti tetua, pemimpin klan, hakim, dan pahlawan melalui pemujaan roh mereka.",
        "en": "People venerate respected elders, clan chiefs, judges, and heroes by praying to their spirits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c03",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Ертеде ел қорғауға аттанған сарбаздар майданға кіргенде, жекпе-жекке шыққанда немесе намысқа шапқанда руға, алашқа ұран болған аталар аруақтарына сыйынған.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada masa lampau, para prajurit berdoa memohon perlindungan roh leluhur saat memasuki medan pertempuran.",
        "en": "In ancient times, warriors prayed to ancestral spirits when entering battle or engaging in duels."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Қазақтар зират маңынан өтіп бара жатып дұға оқиды, алақанын жатып аруақтарға арнап дұға оқиды, бата жасайды.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bangsa Kazakh melantunkan doa dan memohon berkat bagi aruaq ketika melewati area pekuburan.",
        "en": "Kazakhs recite prayers and seek blessings for the aruaq when passing near graveyards."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Басына күн туып, ел-жұртынан ауып бара жатқандар зираттан бір уыс топырақ ала кетеді.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang yang harus merantau meninggalkan tanah air mengambil segenggam tanah kuburan leluhur sebagai pengingat pelindung.",
        "en": "Those facing hardship who leave their homeland take a handful of earth from the ancestral cemetery."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25506634-c06",
      "source_id": "wd-q25506634-s1",
      "quote": "Бала көтермеген әйелдер, созылмалы сырқаттар аруақтардан қайыр-шапағат тілеп, басына түнейді.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wanita yang mendambakan keturunan serta orang dengan penyakit kronis bermalam di makam untuk memohon pertolongan dari aruaq.",
        "en": "Childless women and those with chronic illnesses spend nights at the graves seeking aid and blessing from the aruaq."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Akkula adalah kuda perang tulpar sakti milik pahlawan Manas dalam seluruh versi epos Kirgiz.",
    "en": "Akkula is the primary winged tulpar and warhorse of epic hero Manas in Kyrgyz tradition.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25598410-c01",
      "wd-q25598410-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kisah epos Manas, Akkula digambarkan sebagai tulpar sejati yang lahir pada hari yang sama dengan Manas dan menjadi penentu kemenangan serta kegemilangan sang pahlawan di medan laga.",
      "en": "In the Manas epic, Akkula is depicted as a true tulpar born on the very same day as Manas, central to his martial valor and victories.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598410-c01",
        "wd-q25598410-c02",
        "wd-q25598410-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kuda legendaris ini memuat unsur-unsur mitis magis tulpar purba, lahir dari keturunan ajaib yang ditakdirkan mendampingi perjuangan akbar Manas.",
      "en": "This legendary horse embodies ancient mythical tulpar traits, born of miraculous lineage destined to accompany Manas's epic struggles.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598410-c04",
        "wd-q25598410-c05",
        "wd-q25598410-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Akkula merupakan lambang tertinggi kesetiaan dan kejayaan ksatria berkuda dalam warisan sastra epos masyarakat Kirgiz.",
    "en": "Akkula stands as the supreme emblem of equine loyalty and martial honor in Kyrgyz oral epic heritage.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25598410-c01",
      "wd-q25598410-c02"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Аккула",
    "language": "ky",

      "en": "light dun or golden-hued horse"
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25598410-c01"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {
    "who": {

      "en": "Primary battle tulpar and faithful companion of hero Manas.",

    "origin": {

      "en": "Miraculous birth from sacred herds in the Kyrgyz steppes.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598410-c05",
        "wd-q25598410-c06"
      ]
    },
    "role": {
      "id": "Menjadi penopang kemenangan tempur dan kebesaran nama Manas.",
      "en": "Securing battle victories and upholding Manas's heroic renown.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598410-c02",
        "wd-q25598410-c03"
      ]
    },
    "famous_for": {
      "id": "Ikatan magis dan takdir kelahirannya yang bertepatan dengan sang penunggang.",
      "en": "Magical bond and destiny shared through birth on the same day as Manas.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598410-c01",
        "wd-q25598410-c04"
      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Para penutur Manas mengisahkan bahwa tanpa Akkula, keperkasaan dan kejayaan Manas sebagai ksatria tidak akan pernah lengkap.",
    "en": "Manas bards recount that without Akkula, the hero's peerless martial feats could never have been accomplished.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q25598410-c02"
    ]
  },
  "abilities": [],

    {

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c01",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Аккула (Каккула, Ак сур ат) — эпостун бардык варианттарында Манастын жанга өлчөлүү негизги тулпары.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Akkula adalah tulpar utama dan kuda perang setia Manas dalam seluruh versi epos.",
        "en": "Akkula is the primary tulpar and faithful warhorse of hero Manas in all variants of the epic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Манас да башка эпикалык каармандардай эле урунган куралы, кийген кийими, жоого минчү жакшы аты менен гана баатыр, ансыз анын даңкы чыкпайт, эрдик сапаттары көрүнбөйт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kehebatan dan kemasyhuran Manas sebagai pahlawan tak terpisahkan dari kuda perangnya yang luar biasa.",
        "en": "The renown and valor of Manas as an epic hero are inseparable from his exceptional warhorse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c03",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Анын эрдиги жана жеңишке ээ болушу Аккулага да байланыштуу.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keberanian serta kemenangan Manas dalam pertempuran berkaitan erat dengan ketangguhan Akkula.",
        "en": "Manas's courage and ultimate victories in battle were closely bound to Akkula's endurance."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Эпостогу каармандарды идеалдаштырууда эпикалык тулпарларга байланыштуу мифтик фантастикалык элементтердин көрүнүштөрү Аккуланын образынан орун алган.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Unsur mistis dan fantastis seputar tulpar epik terwujud nyata dalam gambaran figur Akkula.",
        "en": "Mythical and fantastic elements associated with epic tulpars are embodied within Akkula's character."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Манас төрөлгөн учурда Камбарбоздун үйрүнөн кара жалдуу Кула бээ ак сур кулун тууйт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat Manas lahir, seekor induk kuda bersurai hitam melahirkan anak kuda abu-abu terang yang ditakdirkan menjadi Akkula.",
        "en": "When Manas was born, a black-maned mare gave birth to a light grey foal destined to be Akkula."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q25598410-c06",
      "source_id": "wd-q25598410-s1",
      "quote": "Манасчы Аккуланы кайыптан туулган Куу байталдын Кула кулуну деп сыпаттайт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penutur Manas menggambarkan Akkula sebagai anak kuda berbulu emas kecokelatan yang lahir dari keturunan ajaib.",
        "en": "Manas bards describe Akkula as a miraculous dun foal born of an otherworldly lineage."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

      "en": "In the Kyrgyz epic, Algara is depicted as the fastest tulpar, uncatchable by any pursuer and always catching whoever it pursues.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q25598411-c01",
        "wd-q25598411-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kuda hitam perkasa ini berulang kali menyelamatkan Konurbay dari maut bahkan saat Akkula gagal mengejarnya, hingga gugur bersama tuannya dalam epos Semetey.",
      "en": "This black steed saved Konurbay from death many times when even Akkula fell behind, eventually perishing beside him in Semetey.",

      "quote": "Алгара Коңурбайдын тулпары. Эпостогу эң бир күлүк,куугунга жеткизбеген, кууганына жеткен тулпар.",

      "source_id": "wd-q25598411-s1",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "wd-q25598411-s1",
      "quote": "Семетейде Коңурбай менен чогуу өлөт. Кээбир учурларда кара ат деп сыпатталат.",

      "quote": "Эпостогу эң бир күлүк,куугунга жеткизбеген, кууганына жеткен тулпар.",

    {
      "id": "Dalam agama tradisional suku Mari, roh para leluhur yang dinamai Toshtyen memegang kedudukan sangat penting, dihormati melalui ritual keluarga dan klan agar arwah leluhur tidak mengganggu manusia yang masih hidup.",
      "en": "In Mari traditional religion, ancestral spirits known as Toshtyen hold immense importance, venerated in clan rituals so that ancestors do not afflict the living.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4461617-c01",
        "wd-q4461617-c02",
        "wd-q4461617-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Masyarakat meyakini bahwa penyakit dan kemalangan dapat timbul akibat pembalasan dari arwah leluhur yang terlupakan atau tidak berkerabat.",
      "en": "Illnesses and misfortunes were considered retributions from forgotten or clanless ancestral spirits.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4461617-c04",
        "wd-q4461617-c05",
        "wd-q4461617-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Pemujaan Toshtyen mencerminkan kewajiban moral generasi penerus dalam melestarikan ingatan silsilah dan menghormati arwah para tetua Mari.",
    "en": "Toshtyen veneration exemplifies traditional Mari filial obligations to preserve genealogical memory and honor deceased elders.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q4461617-c01",
      "wd-q4461617-c03"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Тоштыен",
    "language": "tt",
    "literal_meaning": {
      "id": "manusia zaman purba atau leluhur kuno",
      "en": "ancient person or ancestor"
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q4461617-c06"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {

        "wd-q4461617-c02"
      ]
    },
    "origin": {

      "en": "Finno-Ugric animist traditions of the Volga-Ural region.",

        "wd-q4461617-c02"
      ]

    "role": {

      "en": "Receiving memorial prayers and guarding the well-being of descendants.",
      "claim_ids": [

      ]
    },
    "famous_for": {

      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Kata 'Toshtyen' berasal dari bahasa Mari 'toshto' yang berarti kuno dan 'eŋ' yang berarti manusia atau orang.",
    "en": "The name Toshtyen is derived from Mari words 'toshto' (ancient) and 'eŋ' (person).",

    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c01",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "Тоштыен (чирмеш телендәː Тоштыеҥ) — чирмеш мифологиясендә бабалар рухы.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Toshtyen (dalam bahasa Mari: Toshtyeŋ) adalah roh leluhur dalam mitologi suku Mari (Cheremis).",
        "en": "Toshtyen (in Mari language: Toshtyeŋ) is an ancestral spirit in Mari (Cheremis) mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "Чирмешләрнең дини ышануларында бик зур әһәмияткә ия.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Entitas ini memiliki arti yang sangat penting dalam kepercayaan keagamaan tradisional suku Mari.",
        "en": "This entity holds great significance in the traditional religious beliefs of the Mari people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c03",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "Тоштыен ритуалы (искә алу) гаилә һәм ыру дәрәҗәсендә башкарылган, ихтирам белән тулы булган; дога кылучылар тоштыеннарны исәннәргә комачауламаска сораганнар.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ritual penghormatan toshtyen diadakan di tingkat keluarga dan klan, di mana doa dipanjatkan agar para leluhur tidak mengganggu orang hidup.",
        "en": "Memorial rituals for toshtyen were performed at family and clan levels, praying that spirits do not disturb the living."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "Авырулар һәм уңмаулар онытылган бабабаларның яки тумыш дәрәҗәсен белеп булмаганнарның — вектымо-куктымо (көнчыгыш мари телендә.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyakit dan kegagalan dipercaya sebagai bentuk balas dendam dari leluhur yang terlupakan atau tidak teridentifikasi.",
        "en": "Illnesses and misfortunes were viewed as vengeance from forgotten or unidentified ancestors."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "утым), ырусызларның үч алуы булып каралган.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kemalangan juga dipandang sebagai pembalasan dari arwah-arwah yang tidak berklan.",
        "en": "Misfortunes were also considered vengeance from clanless wandering souls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4461617-c06",
      "source_id": "wd-q4461617-s1",
      "quote": "Персонаж исеме ике сүздән тора: «тошто» — борынгы һәм «еҥ» — кеше.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama karakter ini tersusun dari dua kata: 'toshto' (kuno/lama) dan 'eŋ' (orang/manusia).",
        "en": "The name of this character consists of two words: 'toshto' (ancient) and 'eŋ' (person)."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kherle Syr adalah roh langit pembawa kesuburan bumi dalam kepercayaan kuno masyarakat Chuvash.",
    "en": "Kherle Syr is a benevolent celestial fertility spirit in ancient northern Chuvash folklore.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Chuvash utara, Kherle Syr (Herle Syir) dipandang sebagai roh baik pemupuk kesuburan tanah yang bersemayam di langit luas.",
      "en": "In northern Chuvash folklore, Kherle Syr is revered as a benevolent sky-dwelling spirit that fertilizes the earth.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4502809-c01",
        "wd-q4502809-c02",
        "wd-q4502809-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebagai peninggalan kosmologi pagan purba, entitas ini menerima sesaji bubur khusus dari kuali ritual seusai doa persembahan bagi dewa agung Tura.",
      "en": "A relic of ancient pagan worship, it receives ritual porridge offerings following prayers to supreme deity Tura.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4502809-c04",
        "wd-q4502809-c05",
        "wd-q4502809-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Pemujaan Kherle Syr memperlihatkan ritus agraris tradisional bangsa Chuvash untuk menjamin kesuburan ladang dan panen melimpah.",
    "en": "Veneration of Kherle Syr highlights traditional Chuvash agrarian rites ensuring soil fertility and bountiful harvests.",

      "wd-q4502809-c05"

      "quote": "Упоминания Херле Сьыру зафиксировано только у северных чувашей.",

      "quote": "Его представляют в качестве доброго духа, живущего на небесах, однако его образ из-за отсутствия связанных с ним поверьев и мифов остается неопределенным.",

      "quote": "По всей вероятности, он тоже является реликтом старого языческого мира.",

      "statement": {

      "source_id": "wd-q4502809-s1",
      "quote": "При завершении обряда моления Туре приносят жертву Пюлехсе и Херле Сьыру.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",

    "id": "Esrel adalah roh psikopomp pencabut nyawa manusia dalam mitologi tradisional masyarakat Chuvash.",
    "en": "Esrel is the psychopomp spirit and personification of death in traditional Chuvash folklore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q4532797-c01",
      "wd-q4532797-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Chuvash, Esrel (juga dikenal sebagai Chun illi) adalah roh antropomorfik yang mempersonifikasikan akhir kehidupan dan bertugas mencabut jiwa manusia ketika ajal tiba.",
      "en": "In Chuvash lore, Esrel (also known as Chun illi) is an anthropomorphic spirit personifying the end of life, harvesting souls when human lifespan expires.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4532797-c01",
        "wd-q4532797-c02",
        "wd-q4532797-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Roh ini menjemput jiwa manusia menuju alam baka, namun dalam banyak cerita rakyat Chuvash terdapat kisah ksatria atau prajurit cerdik yang berhasil memperdayainya.",
      "en": "This entity escorts souls to the underworld, though many folk tales feature clever mortals who successfully trick the Spirit of Death.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4532797-c04",
        "wd-q4532797-c05",
        "wd-q4532797-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Esrel memadukan figur malaikat maut dengan dongeng rakyat tentang kelicikan manusia menghadapi takdir kematian.",
    "en": "Esrel melds death psychopomp archetypes with folk narratives concerning human wit confronting mortality.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q4532797-c01",
      "wd-q4532797-c05"
    ]
  },

      "en": "Traditional mythology and folklore of the Chuvash people.",

        "wd-q4532797-c05"
      ]
    },
    "role": {
      "id": "Membawa arwah manusia dari dunia fana menuju alam kubur.",
      "en": "Escorting departed human spirits to the otherworld.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4532797-c03",
        "wd-q4532797-c04"
      ]
    },
    "famous_for": {
      "id": "Bisa diperdaya dan diakali oleh manusia cerdik dalam dongeng rakyat.",
      "en": "Being outwitted by clever humans in popular folk tales.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q4532797-c05"
      ]

  "did_you_know": {

    "en": "The name Esrel is etymologically derived from Azrail, the traditional angel of death assimilated into Chuvash folklore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q4532797-c06"
    ]
  },
  "abilities": [],
  "weaknesses": [],
  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-s1",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c01",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "Эсре́ль (чув. Эсрел), также Чун илли́ (чув. Чун илли) — антропоморфный дух-психопомп в чувашской мифологии, персонификация конца жизни.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Esrel (juga dikenal sebagai Chun illi) adalah roh psikopomp antropomorfik dalam mitologi Chuvash, personifikasi akhir kehidupan.",
        "en": "Esrel (also known as Chun illi) is an anthropomorphic psychopomp spirit in Chuvash mythology, personifying the end of life."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "Чун илли) — антропоморфный дух-психопомп в чувашской мифологии, персонификация конца жизни.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai roh pengambil jiwa, Esrel bertindak mencabut nyawa saat ajal tiba.",
        "en": "As a soul-taking spirit, Esrel acts to harvest human souls when the end arrives."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c03",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "Согласно верованиям, когда истекает отведённый Богом (чув. Турӑ) срок жизни, Эсрель является к человеку, чтобы забрать его душу в загробный мир (чув. леш тӗнче).",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan tradisional, ketika masa hidup yang ditentukan telah habis, Esrel akan datang menjemput jiwa manusia.",
        "en": "According to beliefs, when the lifespan allocated expires, Esrel appears to take the soul."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "Турӑ) срок жизни, Эсрель является к человеку, чтобы забрать его душу в загробный мир (чув.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Esrel menampakkan diri kepada manusia untuk menjemput jiwanya dan membawanya menuju alam baka.",
        "en": "Esrel appears to take the human soul away to the underworld realm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "У чувашей существует много поверий, как люди обманывали Духа смерти, например, в сказке «Солдат Иван»:",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Terdapat banyak kisah rakyat di kalangan Chuvash tentang manusia cerdik yang berhasil mengelabui roh kematian ini.",
        "en": "There are numerous Chuvash folk tales about clever people outsmarting the Spirit of Death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q4532797-c06",
      "source_id": "wd-q4532797-s1",
      "quote": "араб. عزرائيل‎, ʿAzrāʾīl — «ангел смерти»",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Esrel berakar dari malaikat maut Azrail dalam tradisi Semitik dan Islam yang terasimilasi dalam folklor.",
        "en": "The name Esrel is etymologically derived from the angel of death Azrail in Semitic traditions."
      }
    }
  ],

}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Sarala adalah kuda perang tulpar sakti milik pahlawan Almambet dalam epos agung Manas.",
    "en": "Sarala is Almambet's battle steed and racing tulpar in the Kyrgyz Manas epic.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q65313108-c01",
      "wd-q65313108-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kisah epos Manas, Sarala digambarkan sebagai tulpar perkasa yang lahir pada hari yang sama dengan pahlawan Almambet, memiliki ikatan takdir dan hubungan batin magis yang sangat erat dengan penunggangnya.",
      "en": "In the Manas epic, Sarala is depicted as a mighty tulpar born on the same day as hero Almambet, sharing a profound magical destiny with its rider.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q65313108-c01",
        "wd-q65313108-c02",
        "wd-q65313108-c03",
        "wd-q65313108-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kuda perang tangguh ini termasyhur atas ketahanannya yang luar biasa tiada kenal lelah, mempertahankan sifat-sifat magis tulpar purba di tengah sengitnya kancah pertempuran.",
      "en": "This battle steed is renowned for its boundless endurance, preserving ancient magical tulpar traits amidst the fury of battle.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q65313108-c05",
        "wd-q65313108-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {

      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "Сарала —  Алмамбеттин  жоого минген жана байгеге чапкан тулпары.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sarala adalah kuda perang tunggangan Almambet sekaligus tulpar pacuan dalam epos Manas.",
        "en": "Sarala is Almambet's battle steed and racing tulpar in the Manas epic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q65313108-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "Эпосто тулпар менен анын ээсинин ортосунда магиялык жагымдуу байланыш бар экени чагылдырылган.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam epos, tercermin ikatan magis yang selaras antara sang tulpar dan pemiliknya.",
        "en": "The epic reflects a harmonious magical bond between the tulpar and its master."
      }
    },
    {

      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "бир күндө төрөлүп, экөө тагдырлаш болуп сүрөттөлөт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuda ini dan penunggangnya digambarkan lahir pada hari yang sama dan memiliki takdir yang bertaut erat.",
        "en": "The horse and its rider are depicted as being born on the same day and sharing an entwined destiny."

    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q65313108-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "Азизкан баласына ат койдурган убакта анын жылкысынан Тору бээ С-ны тууйт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika Azizkan menamai putranya, seekor kuda betina melahirkan anak kuda yang kemudian dinamai Sarala.",
        "en": "When Azizkan named his son, a bay mare gave birth to the foal that became Sarala."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q65313108-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "С-ны сыпаттаганда Манасчылар анын өзгөчө чарчабас-чаалыкпастыгына айрыкча көңүл бөлүшөт.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat mendeskripsikan Sarala, para juru cerita Manas menyoroti ketahanannya yang luar biasa dan tiada kenal lelah.",
        "en": "When describing Sarala, the Manas bards pay special attention to its tireless endurance."

      "source_id": "wd-q65313108-s1",
      "quote": "Бул С-нын образында сакталып калган магиялык касиеттин дагы бир элементи.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Agac Ana adalah dewi pohon purba pelindung pohon kehidupan dalam kosmologi mitologi Turkik.",
    "en": "Agac Ana is the sacred Tree Mother goddess guarding the Tree of Life in Turkic mythology.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q6893371-c01",
      "wd-q6893371-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam kosmologi bangsa Turkik dan Mongol, Agac Ana (dikenal juga dalam bahasa Mongol sebagai Modun Eche) adalah dewi suci yang menjaga pohon kehidupan kosmis pembentang bumi dan langit.",
      "en": "In Turkic and Mongolic cosmology, Agac Ana (revered in Mongolian as Modun Eche) is the goddess guarding the cosmic tree connecting heaven and earth.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q6893371-c01",
        "wd-q6893371-c02",
        "wd-q6893371-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pohon-pohon berongga besar dihormati dalam tradisi rakyat karena dipercaya dihuni oleh dewi pelindung ini, dan beberapa klan meyakini leluhur mereka terlahir dari rongga pohon suci tersebut.",
      "en": "Grand hollow trees were venerated in folk culture as dwellings of this feminine spirit, with various clans tracing their ancestral origins to sacred trees.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q6893371-c04",
        "wd-q6893371-c05",
        "wd-q6893371-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Penghormatan terhadap Agac Ana melambangkan pemujaan alam primordial dan konsep pohon hayat sebagai sumber kesuburan seluruh suku bangsa pengembara.",
    "en": "Veneration of Agac Ana embodies ancient nature worship and the Tree of Life as the fountainhead of nomadic lineage and fertility.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "wd-q6893371-c01",
      "wd-q6893371-c04"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {

    },
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {

      "en": "Primordial tree goddess guarding the cosmic tree and vital fertility.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q6893371-c01",
        "wd-q6893371-c03"
      ]
    },
    "origin": {

        "wd-q6893371-c02"

    "role": {
      "id": "Menjaga keutuhan pohon kehidupan yang menghubungkan alam bumi dan angkasa.",
      "en": "Guarding the integrity of the Tree of Life linking earth to the heavens.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q6893371-c03",
        "wd-q6893371-c06"
      ]

    "famous_for": {
      "id": "Dipercaya bersemayam di dalam rongga pohon-pohon tua yang keramat.",
      "en": "Dwelling inside the hollows of venerated ancient sacred trees.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wd-q6893371-c04",
        "wd-q6893371-c05"
      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Di kalangan bangsa Turkik kuno, pohon-pohon berongga besar sangat dihormati dan dipandang sakral karena diyakini memuat roh keibuan Agac Ana.",
    "en": "Among ancient Turkic tribes, large hollow trees were deeply revered as sacred abodes harboring the maternal presence of Agac Ana.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]

    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c01",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "آغاج آنا - تورک میفولوژی‌سینده آغاج ایلاهه. فرقلی دیللرده آغاچ (آغاش، آغاس، یاغاچ، ییغاچ، جیغاچ، ائوئس) آنا اولاراق دا بیلینر.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Agac Ana adalah dewi pohon dalam mitologi Turkik, dikenal dengan berbagai variasi nama di kalangan suku bangsa Turkik.",
        "en": "Agac Ana is the tree goddess in Turkic mythology, recognized across diverse Turkic dialectal variations."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c02",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "موغولجادا موْد و موْدون (موْدوْن) ائچئ اولاراق دئییلر.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi Mongol, figur dewi pohon ini disebut sebagai Modun Eche.",
        "en": "In Mongolian tradition, this tree deity is addressed as Modun Eche."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c03",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "یئرله گؤیو بیر-بیرینه باغلایان حیات آغاجی اولوقایینی (بای‌تئرئکی) قورویار.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dewi ini melindungi Pohon Kehidupan agung yang menghubungkan alam bumi dengan cakrawala langit.",
        "en": "This goddess guards the sacred Tree of Life bridging the earth and celestial sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c04",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "تورک مدنیتینده بؤیوک و قوووغو اولان آغاج‌لارا حؤرمت ائدیلر، حتّی بو جور آغاج‌لاردان قورخاردیلار، ایچینده آل آناسی‌نین (و یا آغاج آنانین) یاشادیغینا اینانیلیردی.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam budaya Turkik, pohon tua berongga besar sangat dihormati karena dipercaya menjadi tempat bersemayamnya Agac Ana.",
        "en": "In Turkic folk culture, grand hollow trees were venerated as sanctuaries where Agac Ana dwelt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c05",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "تۆکل، تورک خالق اینانجیندا آغاج اؤولادی، آغاجدان دوغولدوغونا اینانیلان آدام، آغاجین ایچیندن چیخان دئمکدیر.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah Tukel merujuk pada keyakinan rakyat mengenai keturunan yang dipercaya terlahir dari rahim pohon suci.",
        "en": "The concept of Tukel signifies folk beliefs regarding persons believed to be miraculously born from sacred trees."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wd-q6893371-c06",
      "source_id": "wd-q6893371-s1",
      "quote": "آغاجین موقدس‌لیگی بئلی‌نین گؤیه دوغرو یوکسمه‌سیندن قایناقلانیر.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kesakralan pohon berakar pada pertumbuhannya yang menjulang tinggi menembus ke arah langit.",
        "en": "The sacredness of the tree stems from its trunk soaring high toward the heavens."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": []
}

      "en": "Meaning literally incomplete body, Yarımtık embodies a capricious demon manifested with only half a physical frame.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Yaztumsuq adalah monster berhidung besi pengubah wujud dalam cerita rakyat Turkik dan Altay.",
    "en": "Yaztumsuq is an iron-nosed shapeshifting monster in Turkic and Altay folklore.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "yaztumsuq-c01",
      "yaztumsuq-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi rakyat Turkik dan Altay, Yaztumsuq (juga dikenal sebagai Castumşuk) adalah monster buas berhidung besi yang dapat menjelma menjadi wanita yang amat jelita.",
      "en": "In Turkic and Altay folklore, Yaztumsuq (also called Castumşuk) is an iron-nosed beast capable of disguising itself as a gorgeous woman.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "yaztumsuq-c01",
        "yaztumsuq-c02",
        "yaztumsuq-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Saat menyamar sebagai wanita, ia menyembunyikan hidungnya; makhluk ini juga digambarkan memiliki lengan dan tubuh sekeras besi serta berkerabat dengan Yaztırnak.",
      "en": "When disguised as a woman, it hides its iron snout; it is said to possess iron limbs and body, closely related to Yaztirnak.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "yaztumsuq-c02",
        "yaztumsuq-c03",
        "yaztumsuq-c04",
        "yaztumsuq-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Yaztumsuq melambangkan peringatan terhadap bahaya yang menyamar dalam keindahan palsu pada cerita rakyat pengelana hutan Altay.",
    "en": "Yaztumsuq personifies pastoral cautions against deceptive beauty concealing predatory danger in Siberian Altay woods.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "yaztumsuq-c01",
      "yaztumsuq-c02"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Yaztumşuq",

      "id": "monster berhidung besi",
      "en": "iron-nosed snout monster"

    "claim_ids": [

    "famous_for": {
      "id": "Hidung dan cakar besinya yang selalu ia sembunyikan dari pandangan.",

        "yaztumsuq-c02",
        "yaztumsuq-c03"
      ]
    }
  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Meskipun mampu mengubah wujud menjadi wanita yang sangat menawan, Yaztumsuq tidak bisa mengubah hidung besinya sehingga selalu menutupi wajahnya.",
    "en": "Though able to transform into an alluring woman, Yaztumsuq cannot alter its iron snout and must always veil its face.",

        "id": "Yaztumşuq adalah monster berhidung besi dalam kepercayaan rakyat Turkik dan Altay.",
        "en": "Yaztumşuq is an iron-nosed monster in Turkic and Altay folk belief."

    },
    {
      "id": "yaztumsuq-c02",
      "source_id": "yaztumsuq-s1",
      "quote": "Qadın qılığında olduqda çox gözəldir lakin burnunu saxlayır.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat menyamar sebagai wanita wujudnya sangat cantik namun ia menyembunyikan hidungnya.",
        "en": "In the guise of a woman it is very beautiful, but it conceals its nose."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yaztumsuq-c03",
      "source_id": "yaztumsuq-s1",
      "quote": "Dəmir qollu, dəmir gövdəli olduğu da deyilər.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dikatakan memiliki lengan dan badan sekeras besi.",
        "en": "It is also said to possess arms and a body of iron."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yaztumsuq-c04",
      "source_id": "yaztumsuq-s1",
      "quote": "Yaztırnak ilə əlaqəli düşünülür.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini dipandang memiliki hubungan erat dengan figur Yaztirnak.",
        "en": "It is considered closely related to the figure of Yaztirnak."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yaztumsuq-c05",
      "source_id": "yaztumsuq-s1",
      "quote": "(Yaz/Yas/Yaş/Cas/Çaş) kökündən törəmişdir.",
      "locator": "Section",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya diturunkan dari akar kata Yaz, Yas, atau Cas.",
        "en": "Derived from the root Yaz, Yas, or Cas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "yaztumsuq-c06",
      "source_id": "yaztumsuq-s1",
      "quote": "Yaztumşuq - türk və altay xalq inancında dəmir burunlu canavar. Castumşuk da deyilir.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster berhidung besi ini juga dikenal dengan sebutan Castumsuk.",
        "en": "This iron-nosed beast is also known as Castumshuk."

    "id": "Yol İyesi adalah roh penjaga jalan dan pelindung musafir dalam kepercayaan bangsa Turkik, Tatar, dan Altay.",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam tradisi rakyat Turkik dan Tatar (dikenal juga dalam bahasa Mongol sebagai Col Ezen), Yol İyesi adalah roh pelindung jalanan yang menjaga keamanan perjalanan para musafir.",

      "claim_ids": [
        "yol-iyesi-c01",
        "yol-iyesi-c02",
        "yol-iyesi-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Masyarakat meyakini setiap lintasan jalan memiliki penjaganya masing-masing, di mana jalan melambangkan keteraturan, hukum, serta kedamaian hidup.",
      "en": "Folk belief maintains that each road possesses its distinct guardian spirit, symbolizing cosmic order, law, and peace.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "yol-iyesi-c04",
        "yol-iyesi-c05",
        "yol-iyesi-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {

    "en": "Reverence for Yol İyesi illustrates nomadic life relying heavily upon safe passage across trade paths and migration routes.",
